<script>
function setGaconnectorHiddenFields() {
    var gaFields = gaconnector.getCookieValues();
    for (var fieldName in gaFields) {
        var selectors = 'form input[name="' + fieldName + '"], form input#' + fieldName + ', form input#field_' + fieldName + ', form input[name="' + fieldName.toLowerCase() + '"], form input#' + fieldName.toLowerCase() + ', form input#field_' + fieldName.toLowerCase() + ', input[value="gaconnector_' + fieldName + '"],';
        selectors += 'form textarea[name="'+fieldName+'"], form textarea#'+fieldName+', form textarea#field_'+fieldName + ', form textarea[name="'+fieldName.toLowerCase()+'"], form textarea#'+fieldName.toLowerCase()+', form textarea#field_'+fieldName.toLowerCase()+', form textarea.'+fieldName+', form textarea[name="param['+fieldName+']"]'+", form textarea[id^='field_"+fieldName+"']";
        var inputs = document.querySelectorAll(selectors);
        if (inputs === null) {
            continue;
        } else if (typeof inputs.length === 'undefined') {
            inputs.value = gaFields[fieldName];
        } else {
            for (var i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) {
                inputs[i].value = gaFields[fieldName];
            }
        }
    }
}

gaconnector.setCallback(setGaconnectorHiddenFields);
setInterval(setGaconnectorHiddenFields, 1000);
</script>

				<h3 class="elementor-post__title">
			<a href="https://www.buildxact.com/us/blog/best-residential-remodeling-software-2026/" >
				Best Residential Remodeling Software (2026)			</a>
		</h3>
				<div class="elementor-post__excerpt">
			<p>Every remodel starts with a margin you plan to make. Keeping that margin intact as material costs fluctuate, change orders add up, and actual costs exceed the ones you forecast is where things get tricky.</p>
<p>For growing remodeling businesses, taking on more jobs means there’s more at stake. A missed line item in the estimate or a cost overrun caught too late can eat into profit a</p>

            const country = formEl.querySelector("#Country");
                if (country) {
                  const row = country.closest(".mktoFormRow");
                  if (row) row.style.display = "none";
                }

            form.onSuccess(function(values,followUpUrl){
                dataLayer.push({event: 'mktoLead', mktoFormId:form.getId});

                //display a thank you message
                //event.preventDefault(); // Prevent actual form submission
                const newsletterDiv = document.querySelector(".inner-newsletter");
                newsletterDiv.innerHTML = "<p>Thank you for subscribing!</p>";
                return false;
            });
        });

    }

</script>

      jQuery('.hover-bg').on('mouseenter', function() {

  <script>
    jQuery(function($) {
      $('.items').hover(function() {
        $(this).addClass('items-hovered', 1000);
      }, function() {
        $(this).removeClass('items-hovered', 1000);
      });

      $('#pricingMenu .tab-title').click(function() {
        var tab_id = $(this).attr('data-tab');
        $('#pricingMenu .tab-title').removeClass('active');
        $('#pricingTabContent .contents').removeClass('active');
        $(this).addClass('active');
        $('#' + tab_id).addClass('active');
      });

      $('#pricingMenu2 .tab-title').click(function() {
        var tab_id = $(this).attr('data-tab');
        $('#pricingMenu2 .tab-title').removeClass('active');
        $('#pricingTabContent .contents').removeClass('active');
        $(this).addClass('active');
        $('#' + tab_id).addClass('active');
      });

       $('.feat-show').click(function() {
        $(this).next('.sub-details').toggleClass('d-block', 1000);
        $(this).next().next('.sub-details-trial').toggleClass('d-block', 1000);
      });

       $('.feat-show').css('cursor', 'pointer');

      $('#brsMenu .tab-title').click(function() {
        var tab_id = $(this).attr('data-tab');
        $('#brsMenu .tab-title').removeClass('active');
        $('#brsTabContent .contents').removeClass('active');
        $(this).addClass('active');

          }
        }, 500);
      }
    });

  <script>
    (function () {
      var isMobile = window.matchMedia("(max-width: 767px)").matches;
      var isUS     = location.pathname.startsWith('/us/');
      if (!isMobile || !isUS) return;

      // NEW destination (single base)
      var NEW_BASE = "https://app.buildxact.com/us/go-signup.html";

      // OLD bases that should be redirected to NEW_BASE
      var OLD_BASES = [
        "https://app.buildxact.com/us/signup.html",
        "https://app.buildxact.com/signup.html",
        "https://app.buildxact.com/us/signup2.html",
        "https://app.buildxact.com/signup2.html",
        "https://stage.buildxact.com/us/trial-for-free/",
        "https://www.buildxact.com/us/trial-for-free/",
        "https://www.buildxact.com/trial-for-free/"
      ];

      var EXCLUDED_HASH = "#demo-section";

      function replaceUrlPreserveParams(url) {
        try {
          var parsed = new URL(url);


          //do not touch for #demo-section
          if (parsed.hash === EXCLUDED_HASH) {
            return url;
          }
          var currentBase = parsed.origin + parsed.pathname;

          if (OLD_BASES.includes(currentBase)) {
            return NEW_BASE + parsed.search + parsed.hash;
          }
        } catch (e) {}

        return url;
      }

      function updateElements(root) {
        root.querySelectorAll('a[href]').forEach(function (el) {
          if (el.href)   el.href   = replaceUrlPreserveParams(el.href);
        
        });
      }

      // Initial run
      updateElements(document);

    })();
</script>

<script>
  (function() {
    const FORM_ID = 1214;
    const BASE_URL = "//on.buildxact.com";
    const MUNCHKIN = "416-XFT-176";
    const CONTAINER_ID = "mktoForm_1214_container";

    // Shared global state so Elementor can execute this script multiple times safely
    window.__BX_MKTO = window.__BX_MKTO || {};
    const state = window.__BX_MKTO[FORM_ID] = window.__BX_MKTO[FORM_ID] || {
      injected: false,
      injecting: false
    };

    function popupIsOpen() {
      // Elementor popup wrapper becomes visible when open
      const modal = document.querySelector(".elementor-popup-modal");
      if (!modal) return false;
      const s = getComputedStyle(modal);
      return s.display !== "none" && s.visibility !== "hidden" && s.opacity !== "0";
    }

    function mount() {
      const host = document.getElementById(CONTAINER_ID);
      if (!host) return;

      // If form already exists anywhere inside the host, stop
      if (host.querySelector("form.mktoForm") || host.querySelector("input[name='Email']")) {
        state.injected = true;
        state.injecting = false;
        return;
      }

      // Don't mount until popup is open (but don't rely on host height)
      if (!popupIsOpen()) return;

      // Marketo must exist
      if (typeof MktoForms2 === "undefined") return;

      // Prevent double injection
      if (state.injected || state.injecting) return;
      state.injecting = true;

      // Prevent stacking if Elementor re-runs
      host.innerHTML = "";

      MktoForms2.loadForm(BASE_URL, MUNCHKIN, FORM_ID, function(form) {
        form.getFormElem().appendTo("#" + CONTAINER_ID);

        if (getBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1214") || getBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1206")) {
          document.querySelectorAll(".exit-intent-form-wrap, #elementor-popup-modal-32308").forEach(function (el) {
            el.style.display = "none";
          });
          return;
        }

        // Bind success handler
        form.onSuccess(function() {
          window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
          window.dataLayer.push({ 
            event: "mktoLead", 
            mktoFormId: form.getId() 
          });

          setBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1214", "1", 60);

          const popupDiv = document.querySelector(".inner-popup");
          if (popupDiv) {
            popupDiv.innerHTML = "<p>Thank you for subscribing!</p>";
          }

          return false;
        });

        state.injected = true;
        state.injecting = false;
      });
    }

    // Try immediately (in case popup is already open)
    mount();

    // Retry loop (handles timing / Elementor lifecycle)
    let tries = 0;
    const timer = setInterval(function() {
      tries++;
      mount();
      if (state.injected || tries >= 80) {
        clearInterval(timer); // ~20s
      }
    }, 250);

    // Best trigger: when Elementor reports popup opened
    document.addEventListener("elementor/popup/show", function() {
      mount();
    });
  })();

  (function() {
    const wrapperSelector = '.exit-intent-form-wrap';

    function hideIfKnown() {
      const knownEl = document.querySelector('[data-mkto-known="1"]');
      const wrapper = document.querySelector(wrapperSelector);

      if (knownEl && wrapper) {
        wrapper.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }

    hideIfKnown();

    const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
      if (hideIfKnown()) {
        observer.disconnect(); 
      }
    });
    
    observer.observe(document.body, { 
      childList: true, 
      subtree: true 
    });
  })();
</script>

				const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
				} );
			</script>

<script>
  // Cookie helpers (global)
  window.setBXCookie = function (name, value, days) {
    var d = new Date();
    d.setTime(d.getTime() + (days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
    document.cookie =
      name + "=" + encodeURIComponent(value) +
      "; expires=" + d.toUTCString() +
      "; path=/; SameSite=Lax" +
      (location.protocol === "https:" ? "; Secure" : "");
  };

  window.getBXCookie = function (name) {
    var prefix = name + "=";
    var parts = document.cookie ? document.cookie.split("; ") : [];
    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
      if (parts[i].indexOf(prefix) === 0) return parts[i].slice(prefix.length);
    }
    return null;
  };

  window.hideIfBXCookie = function (cookieName, selector) {
    if (!window.getBXCookie(cookieName)) return;
    document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(function (el) {
      el.style.display = "none";
    });
  };
</script>


<script>
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
    // Hide whole container if cookie exists
    hideIfBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1214", ".exit-intent-form-wrap");
		hideIfBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1206", ".blog-email-form-wrap");
		hideIfBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1206", ".email-banner-form-wrap");

    // Or hide just the form
    // hideIfBXCookie("bx_mkto_submitted_1214", "#mktoForm_1214");
  });
</script>

<script>
(() => {
  const POPUP_ID = 32308;
  const COOKIES = ["bx_mkto_submitted_1214","bx_mkto_submitted_1206"];

  // --- 1) Don’t run in Elementor editor / preview / wp-admin ---
  const isElementorContext = () => {
    const body = document.body;

    // Elementor editor UI / preview often sets these
    if (body && (body.classList.contains("elementor-editor-active") ||
                 body.classList.contains("elementor-editor-preview"))) {
      return true;
    }

    // URL flags commonly present in Elementor edit/preview/canvas
    const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    if (qs.has("elementor-preview") || qs.get("action") === "elementor") return true;
    if (qs.get("elementor_library")) return true;

    // wp-admin (just in case this script is injected globally)
    if (location.pathname.startsWith("/wp-admin")) return true;

    return false;
  };

  if (isElementorContext()) return;

  // --- 2) Cookie / known-lead gate ---
  const hasCookie = n =>
    document.cookie.split(";").some(c => c.trim().startsWith(n + "="));

  const blocked = () =>
    COOKIES.some(hasCookie) || document.querySelector('[data-mkto-known="1"]');

  // --- 3) Helpers ---
  const getPopupId = (modal) => {
    const idAttr = modal?.id || "";                 // e.g. elementor-popup-modal-28392
    const m = idAttr.match(/(\d+)/);
    if (m) return Number(m[1]);

    const dataId = modal?.getAttribute?.("data-id");
    if (dataId) return Number(dataId);

    return NaN;
  };

  const hideImmediately = (modal) => {
    // Reduce flicker before close
    modal.style.visibility = "hidden";
    modal.style.opacity = "0";
    modal.style.pointerEvents = "none";
    modal.style.transition = "none";
  };

  const closeModal = (modal) => {
    // Try click close button first (clean close)
    const btn = modal.querySelector(".dialog-close-button, .dialog-close-button-link");
    if (btn) {
      btn.click();
      return;
    }

    // Fallback: force hide
    modal.style.display = "none";
    modal.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    document.documentElement.classList.remove("elementor-popup-modal-open");
    document.body.classList.remove("elementor-popup-modal-open");
  };

  // --- 4) Exit intent blocker (prevents trigger) ---
  document.addEventListener("mouseout", e => {
    if (e.relatedTarget || e.clientY > 0) return;
    if (!blocked()) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
  }, true);

  // --- 5) Close popup as soon as it appears, with minimal flicker ---
  const handleExisting = () => {
    if (!blocked()) return;
    document.querySelectorAll(".elementor-popup-modal").forEach(modal => {
      if (getPopupId(modal) === POPUP_ID) {
        hideImmediately(modal);
        closeModal(modal);
      }
    });
  };

  // Run once in case popup is already in DOM
  handleExisting();

  // Observe future popup injections
  const mo = new MutationObserver(() => handleExisting());
  mo.observe(document.documentElement, { childList: true, subtree: true });
})();
</script>
<script>
(function() {
  function appendImRef() {
    var params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    var imRef = params.get('im_ref');
    if (!imRef) { return; }
    var links = document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="signup.html"], a[href*="signupexp.html"]');
    links.forEach(function(link) {
      try {
        var url = new URL(link.href, window.location.origin);
        if (!url.searchParams.has('im_ref')) {
          url.searchParams.set('im_ref', imRef);
          link.href = url.toString();
				}
			} catch (e) {}
		});
	}
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', appendImRef);
	} else {
    appendImRef();
	}
})();</script>
